</div> <script> document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(event) {
    // Set the date we're counting down to
    var countDownDate=new Date("Oct 23, 2019 23:59:59").getTime();
    // Update the count down every 1 second
    var x=setInterval(function() {
        // Get todays date and time
        var now=new Date().getTime();
        // Find the distance between now an the count down date
        var distance=countDownDate - now;
        // Time calculations for days, hours, minutes and seconds
        var hours=Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) / (1000 * 60 * 60));
        var minutes=Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60 * 60)) / (1000 * 60));
        var seconds=Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60)) / 1000);
        // Output the result in an element
        document.getElementById("hours_live").innerHTML=hours;
        document.getElementById("minutes_live").innerHTML=minutes;
        document.getElementById("seconds_live").innerHTML=seconds;
        // If the count down is over, write some text
        if (distance < 0) {
            clearInterval(x);
            document.getElementById("hours_live").innerHTML='0';
            document.getElementById("minutes_live").innerHTML='0';
            document.getElementById("seconds_live").innerHTML='0';
        }
    }
    , 100);
}

);
</script><script> document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(event) {

  function test(x) {
    var y = x.replace("X", "");
    var m = document.getElementById("Image" + y).innerHTML;
    document.getElementById("ImageRotation").innerHTML = m;
    var n = document.getElementById("RotationText" + y).innerHTML;
    document.getElementById("ImageRotationText").innerHTML = n;
    var o = document.getElementById("ImageRotationColorLink" + y).innerHTML;
    document.getElementById("ImageRotationCta").innerHTML = o;
    if (x.indexOf("X") > 0) {
      document.getElementById("ImageRotationColor").style.display = "none";
    } else {
      document.getElementById("ImageRotationColor").style.display = "block";
      var p = document.getElementById("ImageColor" + y).innerHTML;
      document.getElementById("ImageRotationColor").innerHTML = p
    }
  }

<script type="text/javascript">
  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
  console.log('Your document is ready!',jQuery);
  $ = jQuery;
  $(document).on('click', '.swiper-slide', function() {
    $('.swiper-slide').removeClass("active");
    $(this).addClass("active");
  });
  $(document).on("click", ".js-videoPoster", function(d) {
    d.preventDefault();
    var e = $(this);
    var f = e.closest(".js-videoWrapper");
    videoPlay(f)
  });
  })
  </script><script type="text/javascript">

  // function display(b) {
  //   $("#swiper-wrapper-container").children("*").show();
  //   $("#" + b).hide()
  // }

  function myFunction(g) {
    var h = document.getElementById("video" + g).innerHTML;
    document.getElementById("vid_frame").innerHTML = h;
    var e = document.getElementById("context" + g).innerHTML;
    document.getElementById("context").innerHTML = e;
    var f = document.getElementById("datalink" + g).innerHTML;
    document.getElementById("content-cta").innerHTML = f
  }

  function videoPlay(e) {
    var d = e.find(".js-videoIframe");
    var f = d.data("src");
    e.addClass("videoWrapperActive");
    d.attr("src", f);
    $(".home-video-close").removeClass("is-hidden")
  }

  function videoStop(d) {
    if (!d) {
      var d = $(".js-videoWrapper");
      var c = $(".js-videoIframe")
    } else {
      var c = d.find(".js-videoIframe")
    }
    d.removeClass("videoWrapperActive");
    c.attr("src", "");
    $(".home-video-close").addClass("is-hidden")
  }
  </script>
